| 2026-11-15 13:52 | — | — | — | — | DECISION NEEDED (J. Picione): both sides ended by the stopping rule at frame 20, only 0.36 kJ/mol (forward) and 0.42 kJ/mol (backward) below the TS. The TS is the flat 92i cm⁻¹ C2–C3 rotation TS: the first 20 steps each change the energy by < 0.05 kJ/mol and no key bond changes, so the rule (20 flat steps after the last bond event) is met while the path is still leaving the TS. The sides end 15.4–15.5 kJ/mol above secondary_eclip. Optimized from the ends: forward reaches the tertiary cation (C2–H 1.117 Å formed, C3–H 1.992 Å; +0.117 kJ/mol from `tertiary`), backward reaches secondary_eclip (+0.039 kJ/mol, heavy-atom RMSD 0.052 Å). So the TS does connect the secondary and tertiary cations, but the path covers only the TS region. Not presented as the hydride shift path until he decides. |
